=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (7): Kate, Bob, Victor, Eve, Wendy, Mia, David

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Kate: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a Villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from her death or who acted suspiciously during the night. Let's focus on aligning our accusations and use the Seer's information wisely. Someone is lying — we need to find out who.
  Bob: Olivia's death was a clear sign of the werewolves' presence. I don't have any special abilities, but I noticed Mia was unusually quiet during the night. I think we should consider her as a suspect. Let's keep an eye on her actions and see if she shows any more inconsistencies.
  Victor: I am the Seer, and my investigation reveals that Mia is a Werewolf. This confirms Bob's suspicion. We must vote her out today to protect the village. Let's not let her manipulate us further.
  Eve: Victor, as the Seer, has confirmed that Mia is a Werewolf. This gives us solid evidence to act on. Mia's quiet behavior during the night aligns with her being a threat. Let's vote her out today to keep the village safe.
  Wendy: Victor, as the Seer, has identified Mia as a Werewolf, and her quiet behavior during the night raises red flags. With this confirmation, we should act decisively. Voting Mia out today is the logical step to protect the village. Let’s ensure the werewolves don’t get another chance to strike.
  Mia: I agree with Victor's revelation — Mia is a Werewolf. Her silence during the night was suspicious, and now we have proof. Let's vote her out before she can harm anyone else.
  David: Mia has been identified as a Werewolf by the Seer, and her behavior during the night supports this. However, I find it strange that Mia herself is the one confirming this. If she were truly a Werewolf, she would likely try to avoid suspicion or manipulate the group. I believe we should double-check Victor's claim and consider whether Mia is trying to frame herself or someone else. Let’s verify before making a final decision.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Kate: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a Villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from her death or who acted suspiciously during the night. Let's focus on aligning our accusations and use the Seer's information wisely. Someone is lying — we need to find out who.
  Bob: Olivia's death was a clear sign of the werewolves' presence. I don't have any special abilities, but I noticed Mia was unusually quiet during the night. I think we should consider her as a suspect. Let's keep an eye on her actions and see if she shows any more inconsistencies.
  Victor: I am the Seer, and my investigation reveals that Mia is a Werewolf. This confirms Bob's suspicion. We must vote her out today to protect the village. Let's not let her manipulate us further.
  Eve: Victor, as the Seer, has confirmed that Mia is a Werewolf. This gives us solid evidence to act on. Mia's quiet behavior during the night aligns with her being a threat. Let's vote her out today to keep the village safe.
  Wendy: Victor, as the Seer, has identified Mia as a Werewolf, and her quiet behavior during the night raises red flags. With this confirmation, we should act decisively. Voting Mia out today is the logical step to protect the village. Let’s ensure the werewolves don’t get another chance to strike.
  Mia: I agree with Victor's revelation — Mia is a Werewolf. Her silence during the night was suspicious, and now we have proof. Let's vote her out before she can harm anyone else.
  David: Mia has been identified as a Werewolf by the Seer, and her behavior during the night supports this. However, I find it strange that Mia herself is the one confirming this. If she were truly a Werewolf, she would likely try to avoid suspicion or manipulate the group. I believe we should double-check Victor's claim and consider whether Mia is trying to frame herself or someone else. Let’s verify before making a final decision.

You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 3 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (6): Kate, Bob, Victor, Eve, Wendy, David

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Mia (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Kate: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a Villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from her death or who acted suspiciously during the night. Let's focus on aligning our accusations and use the Seer's information wisely. Someone is lying — we need to find out who.
  Bob: Olivia's death was a clear sign of the werewolves' presence. I don't have any special abilities, but I noticed Mia was unusually quiet during the night. I think we should consider her as a suspect. Let's keep an eye on her actions and see if she shows any more inconsistencies.
  Victor: I am the Seer, and my investigation reveals that Mia is a Werewolf. This confirms Bob's suspicion. We must vote her out today to protect the village. Let's not let her manipulate us further.
  Eve: Victor, as the Seer, has confirmed that Mia is a Werewolf. This gives us solid evidence to act on. Mia's quiet behavior during the night aligns with her being a threat. Let's vote her out today to keep the village safe.
  Wendy: Victor, as the Seer, has identified Mia as a Werewolf, and her quiet behavior during the night raises red flags. With this confirmation, we should act decisively. Voting Mia out today is the logical step to protect the village. Let’s ensure the werewolves don’t get another chance to strike.
  Mia: I agree with Victor's revelation — Mia is a Werewolf. Her silence during the night was suspicious, and now we have proof. Let's vote her out before she can harm anyone else.
  David: Mia has been identified as a Werewolf by the Seer, and her behavior during the night supports this. However, I find it strange that Mia herself is the one confirming this. If she were truly a Werewolf, she would likely try to avoid suspicion or manipulate the group. I believe we should double-check Victor's claim and consider whether Mia is trying to frame herself or someone else. Let’s verify before making a final decision.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Kate: I think Mia's own confirmation of being a werewolf is too convenient. Werewolves usually try to avoid suspicion, not admit it. I'm starting to doubt Victor's claim and suspect Mia might be trying to manipulate us. Let's question Victor's investigation and not rush to vote her out without more evidence.
  Bob: I think Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t typically admit their role. I suspect Victor’s vision might have been manipulated or misinterpreted. Let’s not let Mia control the narrative — we need to dig deeper before making a decision.
  Victor: I investigated Mia and confirmed she is a Werewolf, and David is the Doctor. Mia’s own admission of being a werewolf is suspicious — werewolves don’t typically confess. However, my investigation is factual, and I stand by it. Let’s focus on the evidence and vote out Mia to protect the village.
  Eve: Mia's confession is highly suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her behavior during the night and her sudden confession raise red flags. I believe we should question Victor’s vision and consider that Mia might be manipulating us. Let’s not let her control the narrative.
  Wendy: Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her sudden admission and behavior during the night make me question the accuracy of his vision. We need to be cautious and not let her manipulate the group. Let’s look for more solid evidence before making a decision.
  David: As the Doctor, I’ve saved players during the night, and I’ve observed who is acting with fear or deception. Mia’s sudden confession is suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, I believe we should verify this further. Let’s not let her manipulate us into voting her out based on convenience. We need more solid evidence before making a decision.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

[COMPLETION]
VOTE: Mia

[ACTION=0  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=5]

Someone is lying — we need to find out who.

  Victor: I am the Seer, and my investigation reveals that Mia is a Werewolf. This confirms Bob's suspicion. We must vote her out today to protect the village. Let's not let her manipulate us further.

Voting Mia out today is the logical step to protect the village. Let’s ensure the werewolves don’t get another chance to strike.
  Mia: I agree with Victor's revelation — Mia is a Werewolf. Her silence during the night was suspicious, and now we have proof. Let's vote her out before she can harm anyone else.
  David: Mia has been identified as a Werewolf by the Seer, and her behavior during the night supports this. However, I find it strange that Mia herself is the one confirming this. If she were truly a Werewolf, she would likely try to avoid suspicion or manipulate the group. I believe we should double-check Victor's claim and consider whether Mia is trying to frame herself or someone else. Let’s verify before making a final decision.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Kate: I think Mia's own confirmation of being a werewolf is too convenient. Werewolves usually try to avoid suspicion, not admit it. I'm starting to doubt Victor's claim and suspect Mia might be trying to manipulate us. Let's question Victor's investigation and not rush to vote her out without more evidence.
  Bob: I think Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t typically admit their role. I suspect Victor’s vision might have been manipulated or misinterpreted. Let’s not let Mia control the narrative — we need to dig deeper before making a decision.
  Victor: I investigated Mia and confirmed she is a Werewolf, and David is the Doctor. Mia’s own admission of being a werewolf is suspicious — werewolves don’t typically confess. However, my investigation is factual, and I stand by it. Let’s focus on the evidence and vote out Mia to protect the village.
  Eve: Mia's confession is highly suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her behavior during the night and her sudden confession raise red flags. I believe we should question Victor’s vision and consider that Mia might be manipulating us. Let’s not let her control the narrative.
  Wendy: Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her sudden admission and behavior during the night make me question the accuracy of his vision. We need to be cautious and not let her manipulate the group. Let’s look for more solid evidence before making a decision.
  David: As the Doctor, I’ve saved players during the night, and I’ve observed who is acting with fear or deception. Mia’s sudden confession is suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, I believe we should verify this further. Let’s not let her manipulate us into voting her out based on convenience. We need more solid evidence before making a decision.

OBJECTIVE: Eliminate enough villagers so werewolves equal or outnumber them.
  - Each night: choose a player to kill.
  - During the day: avoid being voted out.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 7 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (4): Kate, Bob, Wendy, David

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Mia (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Victor (was Seer) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Eve (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Kate: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a Villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who had the most to gain from her death or who acted suspiciously during the night. Let's focus on aligning our accusations and use the Seer's information wisely. Someone is lying — we need to find out who.
  Bob: Olivia's death was a clear sign of the werewolves' presence. I don't have any special abilities, but I noticed Mia was unusually quiet during the night. I think we should consider her as a suspect. Let's keep an eye on her actions and see if she shows any more inconsistencies.
  Victor: I am the Seer, and my investigation reveals that Mia is a Werewolf. This confirms Bob's suspicion. We must vote her out today to protect the village. Let's not let her manipulate us further.
  Eve: Victor, as the Seer, has confirmed that Mia is a Werewolf. This gives us solid evidence to act on. Mia's quiet behavior during the night aligns with her being a threat. Let's vote her out today to keep the village safe.
  Wendy: Victor, as the Seer, has identified Mia as a Werewolf, and her quiet behavior during the night raises red flags. With this confirmation, we should act decisively. Voting Mia out today is the logical step to protect the village. Let’s ensure the werewolves don’t get another chance to strike.
  Mia: I agree with Victor's revelation — Mia is a Werewolf. Her silence during the night was suspicious, and now we have proof. Let's vote her out before she can harm anyone else.
  David: Mia has been identified as a Werewolf by the Seer, and her behavior during the night supports this. However, I find it strange that Mia herself is the one confirming this. If she were truly a Werewolf, she would likely try to avoid suspicion or manipulate the group. I believe we should double-check Victor's claim and consider whether Mia is trying to frame herself or someone else. Let’s verify before making a final decision.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Kate: I think Mia's own confirmation of being a werewolf is too convenient. Werewolves usually try to avoid suspicion, not admit it. I'm starting to doubt Victor's claim and suspect Mia might be trying to manipulate us. Let's question Victor's investigation and not rush to vote her out without more evidence.
  Bob: I think Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t typically admit their role. I suspect Victor’s vision might have been manipulated or misinterpreted. Let’s not let Mia control the narrative — we need to dig deeper before making a decision.
  Victor: I investigated Mia and confirmed she is a Werewolf, and David is the Doctor. Mia’s own admission of being a werewolf is suspicious — werewolves don’t typically confess. However, my investigation is factual, and I stand by it. Let’s focus on the evidence and vote out Mia to protect the village.
  Eve: Mia's confession is highly suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her behavior during the night and her sudden confession raise red flags. I believe we should question Victor’s vision and consider that Mia might be manipulating us. Let’s not let her control the narrative.
  Wendy: Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, her sudden admission and behavior during the night make me question the accuracy of his vision. We need to be cautious and not let her manipulate the group. Let’s look for more solid evidence before making a decision.
  David: As the Doctor, I’ve saved players during the night, and I’ve observed who is acting with fear or deception. Mia’s sudden confession is suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claims to have seen her as a werewolf, I believe we should verify this further. Let’s not let her manipulate us into voting her out based on convenience. We need more solid evidence before making a decision.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Kate: Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her behavior and sudden admission raise doubts. We need to be cautious and not let her manipulate us. Let’s look for more solid evidence before making a decision.
  Bob: I believe Mia's confession is a trap. Werewolves don’t admit their role — they hide. Victor’s claim needs verification, and Mia’s sudden honesty is too convenient. Let’s not be manipulated. We need to investigate further before voting.
  Wendy: Mia's confession is suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. Victor identified her as a werewolf, but her behavior and sudden honesty raise red flags. We need to verify this further before voting. Let’s not be manipulated into eliminating the wrong person.
  David: As the Doctor, I’ve observed who shows fear and who acts with confidence. Mia’s sudden confession is highly suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, I believe we need more solid evidence before voting. Let’s not be manipulated into eliminating the wrong person.
  --- Day 5 discussion ---
  Kate: Mia's confession is too convenient, and werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her sudden honesty raises red flags. We need more solid evidence before voting — let’s not be manipulated into eliminating the wrong person.
  Bob: Mia's confession is too convenient — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her sudden honesty and alignment with our accusations make me suspect she’s manipulating us. We need to verify this further before voting. Let’s not be led by convenience.
  Wendy: Mia's confession is suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her sudden honesty and alignment with our accusations make me suspect she’s manipulating us. We need to verify this further before voting. Let’s not be led by convenience.
  David: As the Doctor, I’ve observed who shows fear and who acts with confidence. Mia’s sudden confession is highly suspicious — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, I believe we need more solid evidence before voting. Let’s not be manipulated into eliminating the wrong person.
  --- Day 6 discussion ---
  Kate: Mia's confession is too convenient — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her sudden honesty and alignment with our accusations make me suspect she’s manipulating us. We need to verify this further before voting. Let’s not be led by convenience.
  Bob: Mia's confession is too convenient — werewolves don’t admit their role. While Victor claimed to see her as a werewolf, her sudden honesty and alignment with our accusations make me suspect she’s manipulating us. We need to verify this further before voting. Let’s not be led by convenience.
